# Bootstrap Self-Host Frontier Tracker

This is the active tracker for bootstrap/self-host frontier work.

It records the serial self-compile frontier after the hosted-header frontier has been cleared.

- After adding an exact bound-template type fast path in

## Status

- `open`: blocker identified, no fix yet
- `in-progress`: reduction or implementation is underway
- `completed`: fix landed, regression placed, validation recorded

## Verification

For each bootstrap child landed on `main`, the minimum validation set is:

- rebuild the touched frontend(s) in `dev/` with `/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++`
- rerun the direct reducer(s)
- rerun the owning assignment suite
- rerun `env CPPGM_HOST_CXX=/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++ make verify-fast-pa10-31 CXX=/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++ VERIFY_ASSIGNMENT_JOBS=6`
- rerun `env CPPGM_HOST_CXX=/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++ make bootstrap-frontier-nobuild CXX=/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++`
- rerun the isolated perf sanity probe `/usr/bin/time -p env CPPGM_HOST_CXX=/usr/local/opt/llvm/bin/clang++ ./dev/cpphostcompat -c dev/src/template_audit.cpp -o /tmp/template_audit.<tag>.o` and reject material regressions before commit
